    <script>
        // Custom Dropdown Logic
        const dropdownSelected = document.getElementById('dropdownSelected');
        const dropdownOptions = document.getElementById('dropdownOptions');
        const gameChoiceInput = document.getElementById('game_choice');
        const optionsList = document.querySelectorAll('.custom-dropdown-option');

        dropdownSelected.addEventListener('click', () => {
            dropdownOptions.classList.toggle('open');
            dropdownSelected.classList.toggle('open');
        });

        function selectGameOption(el) {
            optionsList.forEach(opt => opt.classList.remove('selected'));
            el.classList.add('selected');
            const val = el.getAttribute('data-value');
            gameChoiceInput.value = val;
            
            // update selected UI
            const content = el.querySelector('.dropdown-item-content').innerHTML;
            dropdownSelected.innerHTML = '<div class="dropdown-item-content">' + content + '</div><i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-down" style="color:var(--text-sub);"></i>';
            
            dropdownOptions.classList.remove('open');
            dropdownSelected.classList.remove('open');
        }

        // Init first option
        if(optionsList.length > 0) {
            selectGameOption(optionsList[0]);
        }

        // Type Selection
        const shortlinksConfig = {"link4m":{"name":"Link4m","api_url":"https:\/\/link4m.co\/api-shorten\/v2?api={token}&url={url}","token":"","duration":"12","loop":1}};
        const maxDurationText = document.getElementById('maxDurationText');
        
        function selectType(val, el) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.type-card').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            document.getElementById('server_choice').value = val;
            
            if(shortlinksConfig[val]) {
                maxDurationText.innerText = shortlinksConfig[val].duration + " Giờ";
            }
        }
        
        // init duration text
        let curSrv = document.getElementById('server_choice').value;
        if(shortlinksConfig[curSrv]) {
            maxDurationText.innerText = shortlinksConfig[curSrv].duration + " Giờ";
        }


        // Task Logic
        const totalTasks = document.querySelectorAll('.task-row').length;
        let completedCount = 0;
        const modal = document.getElementById('taskModal');
        const btnVerify = document.getElementById('btnVerify');

        const isTasksEnabled = parseInt("1");
        document.getElementById('btnOpenTasks').addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (totalTasks === 0 || isTasksEnabled === 0) {
                document.getElementById('loader').classList.add('active');
                document.getElementById('mainForm').submit();
            } else {
                modal.classList.add('active');
            }
        });

        function checkTask(id, el) {
            if (!el.classList.contains('done')) {
                el.classList.add('done');
                completedCount++;
            }
            if (completedCount >= totalTasks) {
                btnVerify.disabled = false;
            }
        }

        btnVerify.addEventListener('click', function() {
            if (!this.disabled) {
                modal.classList.remove('active');
                document.getElementById('loader').classList.add('active');
                document.getElementById('mainForm').submit();
            }
        });
    </script>

    <script>
        // Custom Dropdown Logic
        const dropdownSelected = document.getElementById('dropdownSelected');
        const dropdownOptions = document.getElementById('dropdownOptions');
        const gameChoiceInput = document.getElementById('game_choice');
        const optionsList = document.querySelectorAll('.custom-dropdown-option');

        dropdownSelected.addEventListener('click', () => {
            dropdownOptions.classList.toggle('open');
            dropdownSelected.classList.toggle('open');
        });

        function selectGameOption(el) {
            optionsList.forEach(opt => opt.classList.remove('selected'));
            el.classList.add('selected');
            const val = el.getAttribute('data-value');
            gameChoiceInput.value = val;
            
            // update selected UI
            const content = el.querySelector('.dropdown-item-content').innerHTML;
            dropdownSelected.innerHTML = '<div class="dropdown-item-content">' + content + '</div><i class="fa-solid fa-chevron-down" style="color:var(--text-sub);"></i>';
            
            dropdownOptions.classList.remove('open');
            dropdownSelected.classList.remove('open');
        }

        // Init first option
        if(optionsList.length > 0) {
            selectGameOption(optionsList[0]);
        }

        // Type Selection
        const shortlinksConfig = {"link4m":{"name":"Link4m","api_url":"https:\/\/link4m.co\/api-shorten\/v2?api={token}&url={url}","token":"69131ab77e332d0e39098fcc","duration":"20","loop":1}};
        const maxDurationText = document.getElementById('maxDurationText');
        
        function selectType(val, el) {
            document.querySelectorAll('.type-card').forEach(c => c.classList.remove('active'));
            el.classList.add('active');
            document.getElementById('server_choice').value = val;
            
            if(shortlinksConfig[val]) {
                maxDurationText.innerText = shortlinksConfig[val].duration + " Giờ";
            }
        }
        
        // init duration text
        let curSrv = document.getElementById('server_choice').value;
        if(shortlinksConfig[curSrv]) {
            maxDurationText.innerText = shortlinksConfig[curSrv].duration + " Giờ";
        }


        // Task Logic
        const totalTasks = document.querySelectorAll('.task-row').length;
        let completedCount = 0;
        const modal = document.getElementById('taskModal');
        const btnVerify = document.getElementById('btnVerify');

        const isTasksEnabled = parseInt("1");
        document.getElementById('btnOpenTasks').addEventListener('click', () => {
            if (totalTasks === 0 || isTasksEnabled === 0) {
                document.getElementById('loader').classList.add('active');
                document.getElementById('mainForm').submit();
            } else {
                modal.classList.add('active');
            }
        });

        function checkTask(id, el) {
            if (!el.classList.contains('done')) {
                el.classList.add('done');
                completedCount++;
            }
            if (completedCount >= totalTasks) {
                btnVerify.disabled = false;
            }
        }

        btnVerify.addEventListener('click', function() {
            if (!this.disabled) {
                modal.classList.remove('active');
                document.getElementById('loader').classList.add('active');
                document.getElementById('mainForm').submit();
            }
        });
    </script>

<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/three.js/r128/three.min.js"></script>
<script>
(function () {
    var canvas = document.getElementById('dn-sky');
    if (!canvas || typeof THREE === 'undefined') return;

    var reduce = window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    var small  = window.innerWidth < 680;
    var speed  = reduce ? 0.15 : 1;

    var scene  = new THREE.Scene();
    var camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(62, innerWidth / innerHeight, 0.1, 3000);
    camera.position.set(0, 0, 120);

    var renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas: canvas, antialias: !small, alpha: true });
    renderer.setClearColor(0x000000, 0);
    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(devicePixelRatio || 1, 2));
    renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);

    // Poora aasmaan ek group me -> dheere ghoomega (raat me taare ghoomte hain)
    var sky = new THREE.Group();
    sky.position.y = 30;            // taare upar ki taraf
    scene.add(sky);

    // ---- Taare (do layers: dense fine + few bright) ----
    function starLayer(n, size, op, tints) {
        var geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        var pos = new Float32Array(n * 3), col = new Float32Array(n * 3);
        var c = new THREE.Color();
        for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            var r = 600 + Math.random() * 900;
            var th = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
            var ph = Math.acos(Math.random());          // upper hemisphere (aasmaan)
            pos[i*3]   = r * Math.sin(ph) * Math.cos(th);
            pos[i*3+1] = r * Math.cos(ph);
            pos[i*3+2] = r * Math.sin(ph) * Math.sin(th) - 200;
            c.setHex(tints[(Math.random() * tints.length) | 0]);
            col[i*3] = c.r; col[i*3+1] = c.g; col[i*3+2] = c.b;
        }
        geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
        geo.setAttribute('color',    new THREE.BufferAttribute(col, 3));
        return new THREE.Points(geo, new THREE.PointsMaterial({
            size: size, sizeAttenuation: true, vertexColors: true,
            transparent: true, opacity: op, depthWrite: false
        }));
    }
    var fine   = starLayer(small ? 2200 : 4500, 2.0, 0.85, [0xffffff, 0xfff3d6, 0xcfe0ff, 0xd8c6ff]);
    var bright = starLayer(small ? 120 : 260, 4.2, 1.0, [0xffffff, 0xfff0c0, 0xbcd4ff]);
    sky.add(fine); sky.add(bright);

    // ---- Milky Way band (additive points stripe across the sky) ----
    var MW_N = small ? 3000 : 6500;
    var mgeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    var mpos = new Float32Array(MW_N * 3), mcol = new Float32Array(MW_N * 3);
    var mwInner = new THREE.Color(0xfff1d0), mwOuter = new THREE.Color(0x8f7bd6), tmp = new THREE.Color();
    for (var k = 0; k < MW_N; k++) {
        var t = Math.random();
        var along = (t - 0.5) * 2400;                 // band ki lambai
        var across = (Math.random() - 0.5);
        var thick = 130 * (0.4 + 0.6 * Math.random());
        var x = along;
        var y = across * thick + 380;                 // upar ki taraf
        var z = -700 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 220;
        // thoda diagonal tilt
        var ang = 0.35;
        var rx = x * Math.cos(ang) - y * Math.sin(ang);
        var ry = x * Math.sin(ang) + y * Math.cos(ang);
        mpos[k*3] = rx; mpos[k*3+1] = ry; mpos[k*3+2] = z;
        tmp.copy(mwInner).lerp(mwOuter, Math.abs(across));
        var b = 1 - Math.abs(across) * 0.7;
        mcol[k*3] = tmp.r * b; mcol[k*3+1] = tmp.g * b; mcol[k*3+2] = tmp.b * b;
    }
    mgeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(mpos, 3));
    mgeo.setAttribute('color',    new THREE.BufferAttribute(mcol, 3));
    var milky = new THREE.Points(mgeo, new THREE.PointsMaterial({
        size: 3.0, sizeAttenuation: true, vertexColors: true,
        transparent: true, opacity: 0.5, depthWrite: false, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
    }));
    sky.add(milky);

    // ---- Chand (crescent feel: bright disc + soft glow) ----
    var moon = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(16, 32, 32),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xfdf3d0 }));
    moon.position.set(-260, 230, -500);
    sky.add(moon);
    var moonGlow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(28, 24, 24),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xfff1c0, transparent: true, opacity: 0.16 }));
    moonGlow.position.copy(moon.position);
    sky.add(moonGlow);

    // ---- Shooting stars (comets) ----
    var shooters = [];
    function spawnShooter() {
        var geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        var len = 90 + Math.random() * 70;
        var sx = -300 + Math.random() * 500, sy = 250 + Math.random() * 250, sz = -400;
        var dir = new THREE.Vector3(-1, -0.5, 0).normalize();
        var p = new Float32Array([sx, sy, sz, sx + dir.x*len, sy + dir.y*len, sz + dir.z*len]);
        var col = new Float32Array([1,1,1, 1,1,1, 0,0,0]); // head bright, tail fade (3rd unused safety)
        geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(p, 3));
        var line = new THREE.Line(geo, new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({
            color: 0xffffff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.9, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
        }));
        sky.add(line);
        shooters.push({ line: line, dir: dir, life: 0, max: 0.9 + Math.random()*0.5, sp: 520 + Math.random()*260 });
    }
    var nextShoot = 1.5;

    // ---- Resize ----
    window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
        camera.aspect = innerWidth / innerHeight; camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);
    });

    // ---- Mouse parallax (halka) ----
    var mx = 0, my = 0;
    window.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) {
        mx = (e.clientX / innerWidth - 0.5); my = (e.clientY / innerHeight - 0.5);
    }, { passive: true });

    var clock = new THREE.Clock(), running = true;
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () { running = !document.hidden; });

    function frame() {
        requestAnimationFrame(frame);
        if (!running) return;
        var dt = Math.min(clock.getDelta(), 0.05);

        sky.rotation.y += dt * 0.012 * speed;          // taare dheere ghoomte
        milky.material.opacity = 0.42 + Math.sin(clock.elapsedTime * 0.6) * 0.08;
        moonGlow.scale.setScalar(1 + Math.sin(clock.elapsedTime) * 0.04);

        // shooting stars
        if (!reduce) {
            nextShoot -= dt;
            if (nextShoot <= 0 && shooters.length < 3) { spawnShooter(); nextShoot = 2 + Math.random() * 4; }
            for (var i = shooters.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                var s = shooters[i]; s.life += dt;
                s.line.position.addScaledVector(s.dir, s.sp * dt);
                s.line.material.opacity = Math.max(0, 0.9 * (1 - s.life / s.max));
                if (s.life >= s.max) { sky.remove(s.line); s.line.geometry.dispose(); s.line.material.dispose(); shooters.splice(i, 1); }
            }
        }

        camera.position.x += (mx * 16 - camera.position.x) * 0.04;
        camera.position.y += (-my * 8 - camera.position.y) * 0.04;
        camera.lookAt(0, 25, 0);
        renderer.render(scene, camera);
    }
    frame();
})();
</script>    <div class="dl-wrap">

    var small  = window.innerWidth < 680;
    var speed  = reduce ? 0.15 : 1;

    var scene  = new THREE.Scene();
    var camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(62, innerWidth / innerHeight, 0.1, 3000);
    camera.position.set(0, 0, 120);

    var renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas: canvas, antialias: !small, alpha: true });
    renderer.setClearColor(0x000000, 0);
    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(devicePixelRatio || 1, 2));
    renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);

    // Poora aasmaan ek group me -> dheere ghoomega (raat me taare ghoomte hain)
    var sky = new THREE.Group();
    sky.position.y = 30;            // taare upar ki taraf
    scene.add(sky);

    // ---- Taare (do layers: dense fine + few bright) ----
    function starLayer(n, size, op, tints) {
        var geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        var pos = new Float32Array(n * 3), col = new Float32Array(n * 3);
        var c = new THREE.Color();
        for (var i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            var r = 600 + Math.random() * 900;
            var th = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
            var ph = Math.acos(Math.random());          // upper hemisphere (aasmaan)
            pos[i*3]   = r * Math.sin(ph) * Math.cos(th);
            pos[i*3+1] = r * Math.cos(ph);
            pos[i*3+2] = r * Math.sin(ph) * Math.sin(th) - 200;
            c.setHex(tints[(Math.random() * tints.length) | 0]);
            col[i*3] = c.r; col[i*3+1] = c.g; col[i*3+2] = c.b;
        }
        geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
        geo.setAttribute('color',    new THREE.BufferAttribute(col, 3));
        return new THREE.Points(geo, new THREE.PointsMaterial({
            size: size, sizeAttenuation: true, vertexColors: true,
            transparent: true, opacity: op, depthWrite: false
        }));
    }
    var fine   = starLayer(small ? 2200 : 4500, 2.0, 0.85, [0xffffff, 0xfff3d6, 0xcfe0ff, 0xd8c6ff]);
    var bright = starLayer(small ? 120 : 260, 4.2, 1.0, [0xffffff, 0xfff0c0, 0xbcd4ff]);
    sky.add(fine); sky.add(bright);

    // ---- Milky Way band (additive points stripe across the sky) ----
    var MW_N = small ? 3000 : 6500;
    var mgeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    var mpos = new Float32Array(MW_N * 3), mcol = new Float32Array(MW_N * 3);
    var mwInner = new THREE.Color(0xfff1d0), mwOuter = new THREE.Color(0x8f7bd6), tmp = new THREE.Color();
    for (var k = 0; k < MW_N; k++) {
        var t = Math.random();
        var along = (t - 0.5) * 2400;                 // band ki lambai
        var across = (Math.random() - 0.5);
        var thick = 130 * (0.4 + 0.6 * Math.random());
        var x = along;
        var y = across * thick + 380;                 // upar ki taraf
        var z = -700 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 220;
        // thoda diagonal tilt
        var ang = 0.35;
        var rx = x * Math.cos(ang) - y * Math.sin(ang);
        var ry = x * Math.sin(ang) + y * Math.cos(ang);
        mpos[k*3] = rx; mpos[k*3+1] = ry; mpos[k*3+2] = z;
        tmp.copy(mwInner).lerp(mwOuter, Math.abs(across));
        var b = 1 - Math.abs(across) * 0.7;
        mcol[k*3] = tmp.r * b; mcol[k*3+1] = tmp.g * b; mcol[k*3+2] = tmp.b * b;
    }
    mgeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(mpos, 3));
    mgeo.setAttribute('color',    new THREE.BufferAttribute(mcol, 3));
    var milky = new THREE.Points(mgeo, new THREE.PointsMaterial({
        size: 3.0, sizeAttenuation: true, vertexColors: true,
        transparent: true, opacity: 0.5, depthWrite: false, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
    }));
    sky.add(milky);

    // ---- Chand (crescent feel: bright disc + soft glow) ----
    var moon = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(16, 32, 32),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xfdf3d0 }));
    moon.position.set(-260, 230, -500);
    sky.add(moon);
    var moonGlow = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(28, 24, 24),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: 0xfff1c0, transparent: true, opacity: 0.16 }));
    moonGlow.position.copy(moon.position);
    sky.add(moonGlow);

    // ---- Shooting stars (comets) ----
    var shooters = [];
    function spawnShooter() {
        var geo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
        var len = 90 + Math.random() * 70;
        var sx = -300 + Math.random() * 500, sy = 250 + Math.random() * 250, sz = -400;
        var dir = new THREE.Vector3(-1, -0.5, 0).normalize();
        var p = new Float32Array([sx, sy, sz, sx + dir.x*len, sy + dir.y*len, sz + dir.z*len]);
        var col = new Float32Array([1,1,1, 1,1,1, 0,0,0]); // head bright, tail fade (3rd unused safety)
        geo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(p, 3));
        var line = new THREE.Line(geo, new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({
            color: 0xffffff, transparent: true, opacity: 0.9, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
        }));
        sky.add(line);
        shooters.push({ line: line, dir: dir, life: 0, max: 0.9 + Math.random()*0.5, sp: 520 + Math.random()*260 });
    }
    var nextShoot = 1.5;

    // ---- Resize ----
    window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
        camera.aspect = innerWidth / innerHeight; camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);
    });

    // ---- Mouse parallax (halka) ----
    var mx = 0, my = 0;
    window.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) {
        mx = (e.clientX / innerWidth - 0.5); my = (e.clientY / innerHeight - 0.5);
    }, { passive: true });

    var clock = new THREE.Clock(), running = true;
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () { running = !document.hidden; });

    function frame() {
        requestAnimationFrame(frame);
        if (!running) return;
        var dt = Math.min(clock.getDelta(), 0.05);

        sky.rotation.y += dt * 0.012 * speed;          // taare dheere ghoomte
        milky.material.opacity = 0.42 + Math.sin(clock.elapsedTime * 0.6) * 0.08;
        moonGlow.scale.setScalar(1 + Math.sin(clock.elapsedTime) * 0.04);

        // shooting stars
        if (!reduce) {
            nextShoot -= dt;
            if (nextShoot <= 0 && shooters.length < 3) { spawnShooter(); nextShoot = 2 + Math.random() * 4; }
            for (var i = shooters.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                var s = shooters[i]; s.life += dt;
                s.line.position.addScaledVector(s.dir, s.sp * dt);
                s.line.material.opacity = Math.max(0, 0.9 * (1 - s.life / s.max));
                if (s.life >= s.max) { sky.remove(s.line); s.line.geometry.dispose(); s.line.material.dispose(); shooters.splice(i, 1); }
            }
        }

        camera.position.x += (mx * 16 - camera.position.x) * 0.04;
        camera.position.y += (-my * 8 - camera.position.y) * 0.04;
        camera.lookAt(0, 25, 0);
        renderer.render(scene, camera);
    }
    frame();
})();
</script>    <div id="loader"><div class="spin"></div></div>

<style>
    #universe-bg {
        position: fixed; inset: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%;
        z-index: 0; pointer-events: none; display: block;

<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/three.js/r128/three.min.js"></script>
<script>
(function () {
    var canvas = document.getElementById('universe-bg');
    if (!canvas || typeof THREE === 'undefined') return;

    var reduce = window.matchMedia &&
                 window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    var small  = window.innerWidth < 680;
    var speed  = reduce ? 0.15 : 1;          // motion kam karne ke liye

    // Brand colors (style.css se) + realistic planet tones
    var COL = {
        teal:   0x2bffb3,
        blue:   0x5b8cff,
        purple: 0xb56bff,
        gold:   0xffc24b,
        ice:    0xcfe0ff,
        dark:   0x121a3a,   // deep navy 'dark' planet
        ocean:  0x2e6fae,   // realistic blue ocean world (green planet ki jagah)
        mars:   0xb5532e,   // rust / red rocky world
        sand:   0xd9a066,   // tan gas-giant bands
        rock:   0x8a8f9c    // grey asteroid/rocky
    };

    var scene = new THREE.Scene();
    var camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, innerWidth / innerHeight, 0.1, 2000);
    camera.position.set(0, 8, 95);

    var renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas: canvas, antialias: !small, alpha: true });
    renderer.setClearColor(0x000000, 0);     // transparent -> nebula peeche dikhe
    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(devicePixelRatio || 1, 2));
    renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);

    // ---- Lights ----
    scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0x8899ff, 0.55));
    var sunLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xfff0c0, 2.2, 0, 2);
    sunLight.position.set(-30, 18, 10);
    scene.add(sunLight);
    var rim = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x5b8cff, 0.6);
    rim.position.set(40, -20, 30);
    scene.add(rim);

    // ---- Starfield (3D, depth ke saath) ----
    var STAR_N = small ? 1100 : 3000;
    var sgeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    var pos = new Float32Array(STAR_N * 3);
    var scol = new Float32Array(STAR_N * 3);
    var palette = [COL.ice, 0xffffff, COL.teal, COL.blue, COL.purple];
    var c = new THREE.Color();
    for (var i = 0; i < STAR_N; i++) {
        var r = 200 + Math.random() * 700;
        var th = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        var ph = Math.acos(2 * Math.random() - 1);
        pos[i*3]   = r * Math.sin(ph) * Math.cos(th);
        pos[i*3+1] = r * Math.sin(ph) * Math.sin(th);
        pos[i*3+2] = r * Math.cos(ph);
        c.setHex(palette[(Math.random() * palette.length) | 0]);
        scol[i*3] = c.r; scol[i*3+1] = c.g; scol[i*3+2] = c.b;
    }
    sgeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
    sgeo.setAttribute('color',    new THREE.BufferAttribute(scol, 3));
    var stars = new THREE.Points(sgeo, new THREE.PointsMaterial({
        size: 1.7, sizeAttenuation: true, vertexColors: true,
        transparent: true, opacity: 0.9, depthWrite: false
    }));
    scene.add(stars);

    // ---- Spiral galaxy (peeche, dheere ghoomti) ----
    var GAL_N = small ? 4000 : 9000;
    var ggeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    var gpos = new Float32Array(GAL_N * 3);
    var gcol = new Float32Array(GAL_N * 3);
    var arms = 3, armSpread = 0.45, galRadius = small ? 320 : 420;
    var inner = new THREE.Color(0xfff2cc);   // bright core
    var midC  = new THREE.Color(COL.purple);
    var outer = new THREE.Color(COL.blue);
    var tmp = new THREE.Color();
    for (var g = 0; g < GAL_N; g++) {
        var rad = Math.pow(Math.random(), 0.6) * galRadius;
        var arm = (g % arms) / arms * Math.PI * 2;
        var ang = arm + rad * 0.018 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * armSpread;
        var spread = (1 - rad / galRadius) * 26 + 6;
        var gx = Math.cos(ang) * rad + (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread;
        var gz = Math.sin(ang) * rad + (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread;
        var gy = (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread * 0.6;
        gpos[g*3] = gx; gpos[g*3+1] = gy; gpos[g*3+2] = gz;
        var t = rad / galRadius;
        tmp.copy(inner).lerp(midC, Math.min(t * 1.6, 1));
        if (t > 0.5) tmp.lerp(outer, (t - 0.5) * 2);
        gcol[g*3] = tmp.r; gcol[g*3+1] = tmp.g; gcol[g*3+2] = tmp.b;
    }
    ggeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(gpos, 3));
    ggeo.setAttribute('color',    new THREE.BufferAttribute(gcol, 3));
    var galaxy = new THREE.Points(ggeo, new THREE.PointsMaterial({
        size: 2.2, sizeAttenuation: true, vertexColors: true,
        transparent: true, opacity: 0.8, depthWrite: false,
        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
    }));
    galaxy.position.set(120, -70, -480);   // door, peeche, thoda side me
    galaxy.rotation.x = -0.9;
    galaxy.rotation.z = 0.3;
    scene.add(galaxy);

    // ---- Central glowing \"sun\" ----
    var sun = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.SphereGeometry(7, 32, 32),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: COL.gold })
    );
    sun.position.copy(sunLight.position);
    scene.add(sun);
    var glow = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.SphereGeometry(11, 24, 24),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: COL.gold, transparent: true, opacity: 0.14 })
    );
    glow.position.copy(sun.position);
    scene.add(glow);

    // ---- Planet factory ----
    var planets = [];
    function makePlanet(opt) {
        var orbit = new THREE.Group();           // center pe ghoomta hai
        scene.add(orbit);

        var planet = new THREE.Mesh(
            new THREE.SphereGeometry(opt.radius, 40, 40),
            new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
                color: opt.color, roughness: 0.85, metalness: 0.1,
                emissive: opt.color, emissiveIntensity: 0.12
            })
        );
        planet.position.x = opt.dist;
        orbit.add(planet);

        // Faint glow halo (optional) — dark planet ko thoda alag karne ke liye
        if (opt.glowColor) {
            var halo = new THREE.Mesh(
                new THREE.SphereGeometry(opt.radius * 1.18, 24, 24),
                new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: opt.glowColor, transparent: true, opacity: 0.10, side: THREE.BackSide })
            );
            planet.add(halo);
        }

        // Ring (optional)
        if (opt.ring) {
            var ring = new THREE.Mesh(
                new THREE.TorusGeometry(opt.radius * 1.7, opt.radius * 0.12, 2, 80),
                new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: opt.ring, transparent: true, opacity: 0.55, side: THREE.DoubleSide })
            );
            ring.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2.3;
            planet.add(ring);
        }

        // Moons (optional)
        var moons = [];
        (opt.moons || []).forEach(function (m) {
            var mg = new THREE.Group();
            planet.add(mg);
            var moon = new THREE.Mesh(
                new THREE.SphereGeometry(m.r, 18, 18),
                new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: m.color, roughness: 1, emissive: m.color, emissiveIntensity: 0.1 })
            );
            moon.position.x = m.dist;
            mg.add(moon);
            mg.rotation.z = Math.random() * Math.PI;
            moons.push({ grp: mg, speed: m.speed });
        });

        var p = {
            orbit: orbit, planet: planet, moons: moons,
            orbitSpeed: opt.orbitSpeed, spin: opt.spin,
            tilt: opt.tilt || 0
        };
        orbit.rotation.x = p.tilt;
        orbit.rotation.y = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        planets.push(p);
        return p;
    }

    // Ocean-blue world with ice ring + 1 moon (pehle hara/teal tha — ab realistic)
    makePlanet({ radius: 4.2, color: COL.ocean, dist: 34, orbitSpeed: 0.10, spin: 0.4,
                 ring: COL.ice, tilt: 0.25, glowColor: COL.blue,
                 moons: [{ r: 0.9, dist: 8, speed: 0.9, color: COL.ice }] });

    // DARK gas giant (deep navy) with 2 moons
    makePlanet({ radius: 6, color: COL.dark, dist: 58, orbitSpeed: 0.06, spin: 0.3, tilt: -0.18,
                 glowColor: COL.blue,
                 moons: [{ r: 1.1, dist: 11, speed: 0.7, color: 0xffffff },
                         { r: 0.7, dist: 15, speed: 1.1, color: COL.ice }] });

    // Small rust/red rocky world (Mars-like) — pehle purple tha
    makePlanet({ radius: 2.6, color: COL.mars, dist: 20, orbitSpeed: 0.16, spin: 0.6, tilt: 0.4 });

    // Far reddish-gold planet, slow
    makePlanet({ radius: 3.4, color: COL.gold, dist: 86, orbitSpeed: 0.035, spin: 0.25, tilt: 0.1,
                 ring: COL.gold });

    // NEW: banded tan gas-giant with wide ring (universe ko bada/unique banata hai)
    makePlanet({ radius: 5.2, color: COL.sand, dist: 118, orbitSpeed: 0.022, spin: 0.2, tilt: -0.22,
                 ring: COL.sand, glowColor: COL.gold,
                 moons: [{ r: 0.8, dist: 10, speed: 0.8, color: COL.ice }] });

    // ---- Asteroid belt (sun ke around patli halki ring) ----
    var BELT_N = small ? 500 : 1200;
    var bgeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    var bpos = new Float32Array(BELT_N * 3);
    var beltData = [];
    for (var b = 0; b < BELT_N; b++) {
        var br = 70 + Math.random() * 10;
        var ba = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        beltData.push({ r: br, a: ba, sp: 0.02 + Math.random() * 0.03 });
        bpos[b*3]   = Math.cos(ba) * br;
        bpos[b*3+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 3;
        bpos[b*3+2] = Math.sin(ba) * br;
    }
    bgeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(bpos, 3));
    var belt = new THREE.Points(bgeo, new THREE.PointsMaterial({
        color: COL.rock, size: 1.1, sizeAttenuation: true,
        transparent: true, opacity: 0.7, depthWrite: false
    }));
    var beltWrap = new THREE.Group();
    beltWrap.add(belt);
    beltWrap.position.copy(sun.position);
    beltWrap.rotation.x = 0.5;
    scene.add(beltWrap);

    // ---- Comet (ek chhota glowing core + peechhe tail) ----
    var cometWrap = new THREE.Group();
    scene.add(cometWrap);
    var comet = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.1, 16, 16),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: COL.ice })
    );
    comet.position.x = 100;
    cometWrap.add(comet);
    var TAIL_N = 120;
    var tgeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    var tpos = new Float32Array(TAIL_N * 3);
    for (var ti = 0; ti < TAIL_N; ti++) {
        tpos[ti*3] = 100 - ti * 0.9; tpos[ti*3+1] = 0; tpos[ti*3+2] = 0;
    }
    tgeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(tpos, 3));
    var tail = new THREE.Points(tgeo, new THREE.PointsMaterial({
        color: COL.ice, size: 1.6, sizeAttenuation: true,
        transparent: true, opacity: 0.5, depthWrite: false,
        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
    }));
    cometWrap.add(tail);
    cometWrap.rotation.x = 0.7;

    // ---- Mouse parallax ----
    var mx = 0, my = 0, tx = 0, ty = 0;
    window.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) {
        mx = (e.clientX / innerWidth  - 0.5);
        my = (e.clientY / innerHeight - 0.5);
    }, { passive: true });

    // ---- Resize ----
    window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
        camera.aspect = innerWidth / innerHeight;
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);
    });

    // ---- Animate ----
    var clock = new THREE.Clock();
    var running = true;
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () { running = !document.hidden; });

    function frame() {
        requestAnimationFrame(frame);
        if (!running) return;
        var dt = Math.min(clock.getDelta(), 0.05);

        stars.rotation.y += dt * 0.01 * speed;
        galaxy.rotation.y += dt * 0.02 * speed;
        glow.scale.setScalar(1 + Math.sin(clock.elapsedTime * 1.5) * 0.04);

        planets.forEach(function (p) {
            p.orbit.rotation.y += dt * p.orbitSpeed * speed;
            p.planet.rotation.y += dt * p.spin * speed;
            p.moons.forEach(function (mn) { mn.grp.rotation.y += dt * mn.speed * speed; });
        });

        // asteroid belt slow spin + comet wide orbit
        beltWrap.rotation.y += dt * 0.05 * speed;
        cometWrap.rotation.y += dt * 0.12 * speed;

        // smooth camera parallax
        tx += (mx * 12 - tx) * 0.04;
        ty += (-my * 8 - ty) * 0.04;
        camera.position.x += (tx - camera.position.x) * 0.05;
        camera.position.y += (6 + ty - camera.position.y) * 0.05;
        camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);

        renderer.render(scene, camera);
    }
    frame();
})();

/* =====================================================================
   HunterModOfc — "UNIVERSE" admin theme
   One shared stylesheet. Every page (admin, login, dashboards, get-key)
   inherits this look automatically because they all use these same
   class names and CSS variables.
   Cosmic deep-space background + twinkling starfield + nebula glow +
   glassmorphism cards + aurora accents. Pure CSS, no JS needed.
   ===================================================================== */

@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Sora:wght@300;400;500;600;700;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;600;700;800&display=swap');

:root {
    /* Deep-space surfaces */
    --bg-primary:    #05060f;
    --bg-secondary:  #0c1024;
    --bg-card:       rgba(18, 22, 48, 0.55);   /* glass */
    --bg-card-solid: #11152e;
    --border:        rgba(120, 150, 255, 0.16);

    /* Text */
    --text-primary:   #eef1ff;
    --text-secondary: #9aa3c7;
    --text-muted:     #5b6488;

    /* Aurora accent (keeps brand green family, richer + cosmic) */
    --accent:       #2bffb3;
    --accent-2:     #5b8cff;

    <style>
        .welcome-wrap { min-height:100vh; display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; padding:20px; position:relative; z-index:1; }
        .welcome-box { background:var(--bg-card); border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:16px; padding:50px 38px; width:100%; max-width:460px; text-align:center; box-shadow:0 20px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.5); }
        .welcome-box h1 { font-family:'JetBrains Mono',monospace; font-size:2.2rem; font-weight:800; margin-bottom:10px; color:var(--text-primary); }
        .welcome-box h1 span { color:var(--accent); }

        .wbtn { display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:center; gap:10px; padding:14px 20px; border-radius:10px; font-size:1rem; font-weight:600; font-family:'Outfit',sans-serif; text-decoration:none; cursor:pointer; transition:all 0.2s ease; border:1px solid var(--border); }

<body>
    <canvas id="universe-bg" aria-hidden="true"></canvas>
<style>
    #universe-bg {
        position: fixed; inset: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%;
        z-index: 0; pointer-events: none; display: block;
    }
</style>
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/three.js/r128/three.min.js"></script>
<script>
(function () {
    var canvas = document.getElementById('universe-bg');
    if (!canvas || typeof THREE === 'undefined') return;

    var reduce = window.matchMedia &&
                 window.matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
    var small  = window.innerWidth < 680;
    var speed  = reduce ? 0.15 : 1;          // motion kam karne ke liye

    // Brand colors (style.css se) + realistic planet tones
    var COL = {
        teal:   0x2bffb3,
        blue:   0x5b8cff,
        purple: 0xb56bff,
        gold:   0xffc24b,
        ice:    0xcfe0ff,
        dark:   0x121a3a,   // deep navy 'dark' planet
        ocean:  0x2e6fae,   // realistic blue ocean world (green planet ki jagah)
        mars:   0xb5532e,   // rust / red rocky world
        sand:   0xd9a066,   // tan gas-giant bands
        rock:   0x8a8f9c    // grey asteroid/rocky
    };

    var scene = new THREE.Scene();
    var camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(60, innerWidth / innerHeight, 0.1, 2000);
    camera.position.set(0, 8, 95);

    var renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ canvas: canvas, antialias: !small, alpha: true });
    renderer.setClearColor(0x000000, 0);     // transparent -> nebula peeche dikhe
    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(devicePixelRatio || 1, 2));
    renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);

    // ---- Lights ----
    scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0x8899ff, 0.55));
    var sunLight = new THREE.PointLight(0xfff0c0, 2.2, 0, 2);
    sunLight.position.set(-30, 18, 10);
    scene.add(sunLight);
    var rim = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x5b8cff, 0.6);
    rim.position.set(40, -20, 30);
    scene.add(rim);

    // ---- Starfield (3D, depth ke saath) ----
    var STAR_N = small ? 1100 : 3000;
    var sgeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    var pos = new Float32Array(STAR_N * 3);
    var scol = new Float32Array(STAR_N * 3);
    var palette = [COL.ice, 0xffffff, COL.teal, COL.blue, COL.purple];
    var c = new THREE.Color();
    for (var i = 0; i < STAR_N; i++) {
        var r = 200 + Math.random() * 700;
        var th = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        var ph = Math.acos(2 * Math.random() - 1);
        pos[i*3]   = r * Math.sin(ph) * Math.cos(th);
        pos[i*3+1] = r * Math.sin(ph) * Math.sin(th);
        pos[i*3+2] = r * Math.cos(ph);
        c.setHex(palette[(Math.random() * palette.length) | 0]);
        scol[i*3] = c.r; scol[i*3+1] = c.g; scol[i*3+2] = c.b;
    }
    sgeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos, 3));
    sgeo.setAttribute('color',    new THREE.BufferAttribute(scol, 3));
    var stars = new THREE.Points(sgeo, new THREE.PointsMaterial({
        size: 1.7, sizeAttenuation: true, vertexColors: true,
        transparent: true, opacity: 0.9, depthWrite: false
    }));
    scene.add(stars);

    // ---- Spiral galaxy (peeche, dheere ghoomti) ----
    var GAL_N = small ? 4000 : 9000;
    var ggeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    var gpos = new Float32Array(GAL_N * 3);
    var gcol = new Float32Array(GAL_N * 3);
    var arms = 3, armSpread = 0.45, galRadius = small ? 320 : 420;
    var inner = new THREE.Color(0xfff2cc);   // bright core
    var midC  = new THREE.Color(COL.purple);
    var outer = new THREE.Color(COL.blue);
    var tmp = new THREE.Color();
    for (var g = 0; g < GAL_N; g++) {
        var rad = Math.pow(Math.random(), 0.6) * galRadius;
        var arm = (g % arms) / arms * Math.PI * 2;
        var ang = arm + rad * 0.018 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * armSpread;
        var spread = (1 - rad / galRadius) * 26 + 6;
        var gx = Math.cos(ang) * rad + (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread;
        var gz = Math.sin(ang) * rad + (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread;
        var gy = (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread * 0.6;
        gpos[g*3] = gx; gpos[g*3+1] = gy; gpos[g*3+2] = gz;
        var t = rad / galRadius;
        tmp.copy(inner).lerp(midC, Math.min(t * 1.6, 1));
        if (t > 0.5) tmp.lerp(outer, (t - 0.5) * 2);
        gcol[g*3] = tmp.r; gcol[g*3+1] = tmp.g; gcol[g*3+2] = tmp.b;
    }
    ggeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(gpos, 3));
    ggeo.setAttribute('color',    new THREE.BufferAttribute(gcol, 3));
    var galaxy = new THREE.Points(ggeo, new THREE.PointsMaterial({
        size: 2.2, sizeAttenuation: true, vertexColors: true,
        transparent: true, opacity: 0.8, depthWrite: false,
        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
    }));
    galaxy.position.set(120, -70, -480);   // door, peeche, thoda side me
    galaxy.rotation.x = -0.9;
    galaxy.rotation.z = 0.3;
    scene.add(galaxy);

    // ---- Central glowing \"sun\" ----
    var sun = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.SphereGeometry(7, 32, 32),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: COL.gold })
    );
    sun.position.copy(sunLight.position);
    scene.add(sun);
    var glow = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.SphereGeometry(11, 24, 24),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: COL.gold, transparent: true, opacity: 0.14 })
    );
    glow.position.copy(sun.position);
    scene.add(glow);

    // ---- Planet factory ----
    var planets = [];
    function makePlanet(opt) {
        var orbit = new THREE.Group();           // center pe ghoomta hai
        scene.add(orbit);

        var planet = new THREE.Mesh(
            new THREE.SphereGeometry(opt.radius, 40, 40),
            new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({
                color: opt.color, roughness: 0.85, metalness: 0.1,
                emissive: opt.color, emissiveIntensity: 0.12
            })
        );
        planet.position.x = opt.dist;
        orbit.add(planet);

        // Faint glow halo (optional) — dark planet ko thoda alag karne ke liye
        if (opt.glowColor) {
            var halo = new THREE.Mesh(
                new THREE.SphereGeometry(opt.radius * 1.18, 24, 24),
                new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: opt.glowColor, transparent: true, opacity: 0.10, side: THREE.BackSide })
            );
            planet.add(halo);
        }

        // Ring (optional)
        if (opt.ring) {
            var ring = new THREE.Mesh(
                new THREE.TorusGeometry(opt.radius * 1.7, opt.radius * 0.12, 2, 80),
                new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: opt.ring, transparent: true, opacity: 0.55, side: THREE.DoubleSide })
            );
            ring.rotation.x = Math.PI / 2.3;
            planet.add(ring);
        }

        // Moons (optional)
        var moons = [];
        (opt.moons || []).forEach(function (m) {
            var mg = new THREE.Group();
            planet.add(mg);
            var moon = new THREE.Mesh(
                new THREE.SphereGeometry(m.r, 18, 18),
                new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: m.color, roughness: 1, emissive: m.color, emissiveIntensity: 0.1 })
            );
            moon.position.x = m.dist;
            mg.add(moon);
            mg.rotation.z = Math.random() * Math.PI;
            moons.push({ grp: mg, speed: m.speed });
        });

        var p = {
            orbit: orbit, planet: planet, moons: moons,
            orbitSpeed: opt.orbitSpeed, spin: opt.spin,
            tilt: opt.tilt || 0
        };
        orbit.rotation.x = p.tilt;
        orbit.rotation.y = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        planets.push(p);
        return p;
    }

    // Ocean-blue world with ice ring + 1 moon (pehle hara/teal tha — ab realistic)
    makePlanet({ radius: 4.2, color: COL.ocean, dist: 34, orbitSpeed: 0.10, spin: 0.4,
                 ring: COL.ice, tilt: 0.25, glowColor: COL.blue,
                 moons: [{ r: 0.9, dist: 8, speed: 0.9, color: COL.ice }] });

    // DARK gas giant (deep navy) with 2 moons
    makePlanet({ radius: 6, color: COL.dark, dist: 58, orbitSpeed: 0.06, spin: 0.3, tilt: -0.18,
                 glowColor: COL.blue,
                 moons: [{ r: 1.1, dist: 11, speed: 0.7, color: 0xffffff },
                         { r: 0.7, dist: 15, speed: 1.1, color: COL.ice }] });

    // Small rust/red rocky world (Mars-like) — pehle purple tha
    makePlanet({ radius: 2.6, color: COL.mars, dist: 20, orbitSpeed: 0.16, spin: 0.6, tilt: 0.4 });

    // Far reddish-gold planet, slow
    makePlanet({ radius: 3.4, color: COL.gold, dist: 86, orbitSpeed: 0.035, spin: 0.25, tilt: 0.1,
                 ring: COL.gold });

    // NEW: banded tan gas-giant with wide ring (universe ko bada/unique banata hai)
    makePlanet({ radius: 5.2, color: COL.sand, dist: 118, orbitSpeed: 0.022, spin: 0.2, tilt: -0.22,
                 ring: COL.sand, glowColor: COL.gold,
                 moons: [{ r: 0.8, dist: 10, speed: 0.8, color: COL.ice }] });

    // ---- Asteroid belt (sun ke around patli halki ring) ----
    var BELT_N = small ? 500 : 1200;
    var bgeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    var bpos = new Float32Array(BELT_N * 3);
    var beltData = [];
    for (var b = 0; b < BELT_N; b++) {
        var br = 70 + Math.random() * 10;
        var ba = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
        beltData.push({ r: br, a: ba, sp: 0.02 + Math.random() * 0.03 });
        bpos[b*3]   = Math.cos(ba) * br;
        bpos[b*3+1] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 3;
        bpos[b*3+2] = Math.sin(ba) * br;
    }
    bgeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(bpos, 3));
    var belt = new THREE.Points(bgeo, new THREE.PointsMaterial({
        color: COL.rock, size: 1.1, sizeAttenuation: true,
        transparent: true, opacity: 0.7, depthWrite: false
    }));
    var beltWrap = new THREE.Group();
    beltWrap.add(belt);
    beltWrap.position.copy(sun.position);
    beltWrap.rotation.x = 0.5;
    scene.add(beltWrap);

    // ---- Comet (ek chhota glowing core + peechhe tail) ----
    var cometWrap = new THREE.Group();
    scene.add(cometWrap);
    var comet = new THREE.Mesh(
        new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.1, 16, 16),
        new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: COL.ice })
    );
    comet.position.x = 100;
    cometWrap.add(comet);
    var TAIL_N = 120;
    var tgeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
    var tpos = new Float32Array(TAIL_N * 3);
    for (var ti = 0; ti < TAIL_N; ti++) {
        tpos[ti*3] = 100 - ti * 0.9; tpos[ti*3+1] = 0; tpos[ti*3+2] = 0;
    }
    tgeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(tpos, 3));
    var tail = new THREE.Points(tgeo, new THREE.PointsMaterial({
        color: COL.ice, size: 1.6, sizeAttenuation: true,
        transparent: true, opacity: 0.5, depthWrite: false,
        blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending
    }));
    cometWrap.add(tail);
    cometWrap.rotation.x = 0.7;

    // ---- Mouse parallax ----
    var mx = 0, my = 0, tx = 0, ty = 0;
    window.addEventListener('mousemove', function (e) {
        mx = (e.clientX / innerWidth  - 0.5);
        my = (e.clientY / innerHeight - 0.5);
    }, { passive: true });

    // ---- Resize ----
    window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
        camera.aspect = innerWidth / innerHeight;
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
        renderer.setSize(innerWidth, innerHeight);
    });

    // ---- Animate ----
    var clock = new THREE.Clock();
    var running = true;
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', function () { running = !document.hidden; });

    function frame() {
        requestAnimationFrame(frame);
        if (!running) return;
        var dt = Math.min(clock.getDelta(), 0.05);

        stars.rotation.y += dt * 0.01 * speed;
        galaxy.rotation.y += dt * 0.02 * speed;
        glow.scale.setScalar(1 + Math.sin(clock.elapsedTime * 1.5) * 0.04);

        planets.forEach(function (p) {
            p.orbit.rotation.y += dt * p.orbitSpeed * speed;
            p.planet.rotation.y += dt * p.spin * speed;
            p.moons.forEach(function (mn) { mn.grp.rotation.y += dt * mn.speed * speed; });
        });

        // asteroid belt slow spin + comet wide orbit
        beltWrap.rotation.y += dt * 0.05 * speed;
        cometWrap.rotation.y += dt * 0.12 * speed;

        // smooth camera parallax
        tx += (mx * 12 - tx) * 0.04;
        ty += (-my * 8 - ty) * 0.04;
        camera.position.x += (tx - camera.position.x) * 0.05;
        camera.position.y += (6 + ty - camera.position.y) * 0.05;
        camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0);

        renderer.render(scene, camera);
    }
    frame();
})();

<script>
    // Redirect if already logged in
    if (localStorage.getItem('fampay_token')) {
        const role = localStorage.getItem('fampay_role');
        window.location.href = role === 'admin' ? 'admin.html' : 'dashboard.html';
    }

    function switchTab(tab) {
        document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
        document.querySelectorAll('.view').forEach(v => v.classList.remove('active'));
        
        if(tab === 'login') {
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab')[0].classList.add('active');
            document.getElementById('login-view').classList.add('active');
        } else {
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab')[1].classList.add('active');
            document.getElementById('register-view').classList.add('active');
        }
        document.getElementById('msgBox').style.display = 'none';
    }

    async function handleAuth(e, type) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const msgBox = document.getElementById('msgBox');
        msgBox.style.display = 'none';
        msgBox.className = 'msg';
        
        const btn = document.getElementById(type === 'login' ? 'login-btn' : 'reg-btn');
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.innerText = 'Please wait...';

        const data = type === 'login' ? {
            username: document.getElementById('login-user').value,
            password: document.getElementById('login-pass').value
        } : {
            username: document.getElementById('reg-user').value,
            email: document.getElementById('reg-email').value,
            password: document.getElementById('reg-pass').value
        };

        try {
            const res = await fetch(`/api/auth/${type}`, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify(data)
            });
            const result = await res.json();

            if (result.success) {
                if (type === 'login') {
                    localStorage.setItem('fampay_token', result.token);
                    localStorage.setItem('fampay_role', result.user.role);
                    window.location.href = result.user.role === 'admin' ? 'admin.html' : 'dashboard.html';
                } else {
                    msgBox.classList.add('success');
                    msgBox.innerText = 'Account created! Please login.';
                    msgBox.style.display = 'block';
                    setTimeout(() => switchTab('login'), 2000);
                }
            } else {
                msgBox.classList.add('error');
                msgBox.innerText = result.message || 'An error occurred';
                msgBox.style.display = 'block';
            }
        } catch (err) {
            msgBox.classList.add('error');
            msgBox.innerText = 'Network error. Please check backend.';
            msgBox.style.display = 'block';
        }

        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.innerText = type === 'login' ? 'Login to Dashboard' : 'Create Account';
    }
</script>
</body>
</html>

            <button type="submit" class="btn-full" id="reg-btn">Create Account</button>
        </form>
    </div>

    <div id="msgBox" class="msg"></div>
</div>

<script>
    // Redirect if already logged in
    if (localStorage.getItem('fampay_token')) {
        const role = localStorage.getItem('fampay_role');
        window.location.href = role === 'admin' ? 'admin.html' : 'dashboard.html';
    }

    function switchTab(tab) {
        document.querySelectorAll('.tab').forEach(t => t.classList.remove('active'));
        document.querySelectorAll('.view').forEach(v => v.classList.remove('active'));
        
        if(tab === 'login') {
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab')[0].classList.add('active');
            document.getElementById('login-view').classList.add('active');
        } else {
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab')[1].classList.add('active');
            document.getElementById('register-view').classList.add('active');
        }
        document.getElementById('msgBox').style.display = 'none';
    }

    async function handleAuth(e, type) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const msgBox = document.getElementById('msgBox');
        msgBox.style.display = 'none';
        msgBox.className = 'msg';
        
        const btn = document.getElementById(type === 'login' ? 'login-btn' : 'reg-btn');
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.innerText = 'Please wait...';

        const data = type === 'login' ? {
            username: document.getElementById('login-user').value,
            password: document.getElementById('login-pass').value
        } : {
            username: document.getElementById('reg-user').value,
            email: document.getElementById('reg-email').value,
            password: document.getElementById('reg-pass').value
        };

        try {
            const res = await fetch(`/api/auth/${type}`, {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify(data)
            });
            const result = await res.json();

            if (result.success) {
                if (type === 'login') {
                    localStorage.setItem('fampay_token', result.token);
                    localStorage.setItem('fampay_role', result.user.role);
                    window.location.href = result.user.role === 'admin' ? 'admin.html' : 'dashboard.html';
                } else {
                    msgBox.classList.add('success');
                    msgBox.innerText = 'Account created! Please login.';
                    msgBox.style.display = 'block';
                    setTimeout(() => switchTab('login'), 2000);
                }
            } else {
                msgBox.classList.add('error');
                msgBox.innerText = result.message || 'An error occurred';
                msgBox.style.display = 'block';
            }
        } catch (err) {
            msgBox.classList.add('error');
            msgBox.innerText = 'Network error. Please check backend.';
            msgBox.style.display = 'block';
        }

        btn.disabled = false;
        btn.innerText = type === 'login' ? 'Login to Dashboard' : 'Create Account';
    }
</script>
</body>
</html>
